# Angolar dictionary

**Language:** Angolar / Ngola  
**ISO 639-3:** aoa  
**Translation pairs:** Angolar → Portuguese; Angolar → English
**Project started:** 23 March 2023  

This file is the **canonical Angolar lexicon**. In this repository it is the principal Angola-related creole (autonym *Ngola*; community name Angolares). Sources locate the language on São Tomé. `data/angola/` is an alias to this dataset, not a second dictionary.

This file is an **Angolar-only** linguistic dataset. It is not a website and not an application.
Forro, Lung’Ie, Cabo Verdean, and Guinea-Bissau words do not belong in these entries.

Entries are isolated Angolar forms from named sources (Rougé & Schang 2012, APiCS Angolar / Maurer 2013, Bandeira 2017, Bandeira, Agostinho & Freitas 2021, and Bandeira & Araujo 2022). Forro files in this repository are not evidence for Angolar.

If a requested Angolar term is not in this dataset:

```json
{"status": "error", "code": "TERM_NOT_FOUND", "message": "Translation not available in the verified Angolar database."}
```

Machine-readable copy: `dictionary.json`. Source record: `sources.md`.
Parent index: `../dictionary.md`.

Spoken APiCS examples are stored in `Audio/` and linked on matching entries.

---

## n'golá

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** noun

### Portuguese
Angolar. Autónimo da língua e da comunidade.

### English
Angolar. Autonym of the language and community.

### Example
Tepu nakulu, no Ngola ka zi kai no kota mionga.

**PT:** Antigamente, nós Angolares fazíamos casas na beira-mar. (gloss APiCS / Maurer)
**EN:** In old times, we Angolares used to build houses by the sea. (APiCS gloss of Maurer 1995)

### Sources
- APiCS Angolar survey (Maurer 2013); example (20) Tepu nakulu, no Ngola ka zi kai no kota mionga.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high


## m'me

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** verb

### Portuguese
Comer. (Maurer: de português comer)

### English
Eat. (Maurer: from Portuguese comer)

### Example
A na ta ngê ki m'me minhu wa.

**PT:** Não se sabe quem comeu o milho.
**EN:** One doesn't know who ate the maize.

### Sources
- APiCS Angolar 36-101. Maurer 1995: 58.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high

## ka

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** particle

### Portuguese
Imperfectivo / futuro.

### English
Imperfective / future.

### Example
Ê kata.

**PT:** Ele/ela cantou. (perfectivo zero, contraste)
**EN:** S/he sang. (zero perfective, contrast with ka / thêka)

### Sources
- APiCS Angolar 36-49 and Table 7. Rougé and Schang 2012: 80: E ka bi.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high


## thêka

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** particle

### Portuguese

**PT:** Ele está a cantar.
**EN:** He is singing.

### Sources
- APiCS Angolar 36-49. Rougé and Schang 2012: 80 write thaka or theka.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high


## ta

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** particle

### Portuguese
Passado. APiCS: Angolar não combina ta com verbos dinâmicos para pluperfeito.

### English
Past. APiCS: Angolar does not combine ta with dynamic verbs for pluperfect.

### Example
Ê ta ka kata.

**PT:** Ele estava a cantar.
**EN:** He was singing.

### Sources
- APiCS Angolar 36-49. Rougé and Schang 2012: 80 tava or ta.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high


## na … wa

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** negation

### Portuguese
Negação descontínua. na pré-verbal, wa no fim.

### English
Discontinuous negation. Preverbal na, final wa.

### Example
e na me lotho wa

**PT:** Ele não comeu arroz.
**EN:** He did not eat rice.

### Sources
- Rougé and Schang 2012: 82. APiCS Angolar 36-101: A na ta ngê ki m'me minhu wa.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high


## ki / ma

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** relative

### Portuguese
ki relativo sujeito. ma relativo objeto.

### English
ki subject relative. ma object relative.

### Example
m kunse ome ki pia mina e

**PT:** Eu conheço o homem que olhou para esta rapariga.
**EN:** I know the man who looked at this girl.

### Sources
- Rougé and Schang 2012: 83-84. Object: m kunse na ma zoze pia.

**Verification:** VERIFIED

**Confidence:** high

## tava

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** particle

### Grammatical information
antériorité (as printed)

### Example
E ta(va) ka bi

### Sources
- Rougé, Jean-Louis; Schang, Emmanuel. 2012. Histoire des créoles et génétique: le cas de l’angolar. Sciences et Techniques du Langage 9. Local files: Forro lan.txt, Forro lan.pdf.

**Verification:** VERIFIED  
**Confidence:** high

**Notes:** Angolar TMA list: ka (imperfectif), thaka ou theka (progressif), tava ou ta (antériorité). Portuguese and English are null because the labels are French. APiCS writes ta; both spellings are kept.


## wa

**Language:** Angolar  
**Part of speech:** negation

### Grammatical information
Second element of discontinuous negation na … wa.

### Example
e na me lotho wa

### Sources
- Rougé, Jean-Louis; Schang, Emmanuel. 2012. Histoire des créoles et génétique: le cas de l’angolar. Sciences et Techniques du Langage 9. Local files: Forro lan.txt, Forro lan.pdf.

**Verification:** VERIFIED  
**Confidence:** high

**Notes:** Angolar-labelled negation example. Interlinear: /il/nég/manger/riz/nég/. French free translation: Il n'a pas mangé de riz. Forro uses fa in the matching example; fa was not copied here. Distinct from Angolar fa ‘to speak’ in other sources.
